### [125] [Generating functions and analytic properties of Apostol-type exponential B-splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26147)
*Damla Gun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文引入生成函数框架，构建了与Apostol-Bernoulli多项式相关的广义指数B样条族，推导了显式生成函数、递推关系及结构恒等式，并建立了参数导数递归公式与频域有理表示，揭示了离散差分算子与指数样条结构之间的谱分析联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成函数技术在组合学和特殊函数理论中广泛应用，但在样条构造中的系统性使用仍然相对有限，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用后向移位算子和基于算子的均匀B样条表示，构建Apostol-Bernoulli型B样条函数；结合de Boor递推关系与微分算子技术，建立参数导数的解析递推公式；通过生成函数的傅里叶变换获得频域有理表示。

Result: 构造了依赖于参数(λ,α)的广义指数样条族，该族在经典多项式样条、指数样条和Apostol型扩展之间插值；得到了显式生成函数、递推关系和结构恒等式；建立了参数导数递推公式；获得了频域有理表示，揭示了离散差分算子与指数样条结构的谱分析联系。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种统一的解析方法来构造广义样条族，为逼近论、基于算子的样条分析和广义谱方法提供了进一步的应用前景。

Abstract: Spline functions, particularly B-splines, play a fundamental role in approximation theory, numerical analysis, and spectral representations. Although generating function techniques are widely used in combinatorics and special function theory, their systematic use in spline constructions remains relatively limited. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a generating function framework for the construction and analysis of generalized exponential B-spline families associated with Apostol-Bernoulli polynomials. Using backward shift operators and operator-based representations of uniform B-splines, we construct new Apostol-Bernoulli-type B-spline functions and derive explicit generating functions, recurrence relations, and structural identities. The present approach also yields generalized exponential spline families depending on the parameters $(λ,α)$, which interpolate between classical polynomial splines, exponential splines, and Apostol-type extensions. Furthermore, by combining de Boor recurrence relations with differential operator techniques, we establish analytic recurrence formulas involving parameter derivatives of the associated spline sequences. We also investigate the Fourier transform of the generating functions and obtain explicit rational-type representations in the frequency domain, revealing a direct connection between discrete difference operators, exponential spline structures, and spectral analytic behavior. These results provide a unified analytic approach for constructing generalized spline families and suggest further applications in approximation theory, operator-based spline analysis, and generalized spectral methods.

</details>


### [126] [Sampling Using Hybrid Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26314)
*Björn Engquist,Kui Ren,Yunan Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合随机动力学的框架，用于从吉布斯分布中采样。该框架在不同区域运行两种不同的采样动力学，通过界面上的自然传输条件耦合，并利用正则化方案建立了指数收敛速率。在径向对称势能下分析了亚稳态性质，表明混合方案能改善平均退出时间，数值实验进一步验证了该优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高从吉布斯分布中采样的效率，特别是在具有亚稳态特性的复杂势能面上，通过混合不同动力学策略来克服单一方法的局限性。

Method: 提出一种混合随机动力学框架，在不同状态空间区域运行两种不同的采样动力学，通过界面上的自然传输条件耦合两者，并构造特殊的正则化方案来保证收敛性。

Result: 建立了混合动力学到平衡态的指数收敛速率；在径向对称势能景观下分析了亚稳态性质，证明混合方案能改善平均退出时间；数值实验进一步确认了该优势。

Conclusion: 混合随机动力学框架能有效提升从吉布斯分布采样的效率，尤其在亚稳态场景下具有显著优势，为复杂势能面的采样问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: This work proposes a framework for sampling from the Gibbs distribution of a given potential using hybrid stochastic dynamics. In this framework, two distinct sampling dynamics are run in different regions of the state space. The two dynamics are coupled across the interface through natural transmission conditions that preserve the target distribution. Using a specially constructed regularization scheme, we establish an exponential rate of convergence for the hybrid dynamics to equilibrium. We also analyze the metastability properties of the hybrid dynamics in a radially symmetric landscape, showing that the hybrid scheme can improve the mean exit time. This advantage is further confirmed by the numerical experiments.

</details>


### [127] [A Fast-Convergence Resolution of the Stochastic Eigenproblem Using Halley's Method and the Spectral-Chaos Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26375)
*Hugo Esquivel,Kabir Oluwatobi Idowu,Guang Lin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Halley方法的谱混沌方法求解随机特征值问题，利用其三阶收敛特性实现了最大收敛率，并发展了张量方法处理随机特征值问题的高维复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机特征值问题对决策、科学知识和工程设计可靠性至关重要，现有数值方法在收敛速度、计算效率和鲁棒性方面仍需改进。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的谱混沌方法，采用Halley方法作为根查找算法（利用其三阶收敛性），并发展了张量方法处理高维随机特征值问题的复杂性。

Result: 该方法实现了随机特征值问题的最大收敛率（由于所得方程组为二次型，无法通过更高阶的Householder方法进一步改进）；在特征向量分量近似已知时优势明显；通过案例验证了Halley方法优于Newton方法和蒙特卡洛模拟。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Halley方法的谱混沌方法在收敛速度、计算效率和鲁棒性方面优于现有方法，为随机特征值问题提供了更高效的求解途径。

Abstract: Solving stochastic eigenvalue problems has long been essential for informed decision-making, advancing scientific knowledge, and ensuring the reliability of engineering designs and applications. This paper underscores the need to continue enhancing existing numerical methods for solving the stochastic eigenproblem in order to improve convergence rates, computational efficiency, and robustness. Specifically, we propose a novel spectral-chaos method for solving the stochastic (linear) eigenvalue problem, employing Halley's method as the root-finding algorithm to leverage its cubic convergence properties. Our method achieves maximal convergence in solving stochastic eigenvalue problems since its rate cannot be further improved using a higher-order Householder method due to the quadratic nature of the resulting system of equations. Additionally, due to the complexity of the resulting system of equations, a tensorial approach was developed to tackle the challenges associated with the dimensional multiplicity of the stochastic eigenvalue problem, without which the solution would have been intractable. The method is derived rigorously, with a detailed error analysis that highlights the benefit of using our approach when the eigenvector components are nearly known, the computational cost of the method is also rigorously presented, and an illustrative example is provided to demonstrate the implementation of the method. Subsequently, a case study is demoed to analyze the results and validate the advantages of using Halley's method over Newton's method and Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [128] [The devil in the (de)tails: an improved recovery guarantee for sparse approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26459)
*Ben Adcock,Simone Brugiapaglia,Avi Gupta*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用样本点i.i.d.结构的新方法，在压缩感知框架下对函数稀疏逼近时，通过用连续L²范数截断误差替代传统L∞范数界来缩小截断字典集，从而降低计算成本，并在加权Wiener空间和各向异性Sobolev空间中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在从i.i.d.点样本进行稀疏逼近时，使用确定性的L∞范数界来约束离散L²截断误差，忽略了样本点的随机性，导致字典截断集n过大，增加了稀疏恢复算法的矩阵规模和计算成本。

Method: 利用样本点的i.i.d.结构，推导离散L²截断误差的新上界，该界反映连续L²范数截断误差的更快速衰减行为，从而允许使用更小的截断集；同时提出改进的稀疏逼近界用于有界Riesz系统，降低了对Riesz常数的依赖。

Result: 在加权Wiener空间和各向异性Sobolev空间中，新方法相比近期工作获得了显著更小的截断集；在有界Riesz系统中，测量条件的Riesz常数依赖更小且具有尺度不变性。

Conclusion: 通过利用样本随机性而非最坏情况界，可以大幅减小截断字典规模并降低计算成本，为高维函数稀疏逼近提供了更高效的框架。

Abstract: Many functions exhibit approximate sparsity in their coefficients with respect to a given dictionary. In recent literature, sparse approximation in such a dictionary from i.i.d. pointwise samples, underpinned by compressed sensing, has become a powerful tool for high-dimensional function approximation. A key step in this framework is truncating the (typically countably-infinite) dictionary to a finite index set of size $n$, so that compressed sensing tools can be used to approximate the function by a sparse combination of these truncated dictionary elements. This introduces a discrete $L^2$-truncation error over the sample points, which in standard approaches, is bounded by the continuous $L^\infty$-norm. Such a deterministic, worst-case bound ignores the randomness of the sample points entirely. As a result, $n$ must be taken unnecessarily large to keep the truncation error under control, which directly inflates the size of the matrix involved in the sparse recovery algorithm and increases computational cost. In this paper, we show that by exploiting the i.i.d. structure of the sample points, the discrete $L^2$ truncation error admits a bound that instead reflects the faster decay behaviour of the continuous $L^2$-norm truncation error and yields significantly smaller truncation sets and decreased computational cost. We demonstrate this through applications to weighted Wiener spaces and anisotropic Sobolev spaces, in each case obtaining significantly smaller truncation sets than recent works. In addition, we also present an improved bound of independent interest for sparse approximation in bounded Riesz systems, where the measurement condition exhibits a smaller (and scale-invariant) dependence on the Riesz constants than in previous works.

</details>


### [129] [Graph-Based ECG Synthesis with Activation-Consistency Certification and Diagnostics-Aware Morphology Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26580)
*Yongjun Yoo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图的心电图合成框架，结合激活一致性认证与诊断感知形态筛选，通过Bellman残差验证激活时间一致性，采用两阶段诊断管线进行质量控制。在心脏图上，RE方法激活时间与Eikonal基准达到近毫秒级一致（R²=0.99876），最终多导联筛选RE接受率高于ET（658/2000 vs 578/2000），形态覆盖率提升至0.09888。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成心电信号需保留可解释的激活、复极和形态特性，以支持算法开发与鲁棒性评估，现有方法缺乏对激活一致性的可计算认证和诊断感知的质量控制机制。

Method: 构建统一心脏图，支持Eikonal-Template（ET）和伪扩散反应-埃克纳尔（RE）两个后端；将图Eikonal激活建模为Bellman不动点问题，以Bellman残差作为激活时间一致性可计算证书；设计两阶段诊断管线，分离度量计算与实验特定接受策略。

Result: RE激活时间与Eikonal基准R²=0.99876；复极实验显示心内膜-心外膜APD梯度决定T波主窗口，扩散强度κ提供次级复极平滑；RE在多导联筛选中接受658/2000样本（ET为578/2000），形态覆盖率从0.09248提升至0.09888。

Conclusion: 该框架为可控、可筛选的合成ECG生成提供了保守的基础，兼顾激活一致性和诊断相关形态质量。

Abstract: Synthetic electrocardiogram (ECG) generation can support algorithm development and robustness evaluation, but simulated signals must preserve interpretable activation, recovery, and morphology properties. We present a graph-based ECG synthesis framework that combines activation-consistency certification with diagnostics-aware morphology curation. A unified heart graph supports an eikonal-template backend (ET) and a pseudo-diffusion reaction--eikonal backend (RE). We formulate graph Eikonal activation as a Bellman fixed-point problem and use the Bellman residual as a computable certificate for activation-time consistency. Each simulated ECG is evaluated by a two-stage diagnostics pipeline that separates metric computation from experiment-specific acceptance policies. On the cardiac graph, RE-derived activation times showed near-millisecond agreement with the Eikonal backbone and achieved $R^2=0.99876$ after causal predecessor filtering. Recovery experiments showed that endo-epicardial APD gradients determined the main T-wave morphology window, whereas the diffusion strength $κ$ provided secondary repolarization smoothing. In final balanced multi-lead curation, RE accepted 658/2000 samples versus 578/2000 for ET and increased per-model morphology coverage from 0.09248 to 0.09888. The framework provides a conservative basis for controllable and curated synthetic ECG generation.

</details>


### [130] [Adaptive Randomized Pivoting for Tensor Singular Value Decomposition Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26688)
*Ahmadsho Akdodshoev,Valentin Leplat,Salman Ahmadi-Asl*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了自适应随机枢轴选择(ARP)从矩阵列子集选择向张量t-乘积框架的扩展，提出了两种构造方法：ARP-T-CUR（傅里叶域切片独立ARP）和T-ARP（全局张量共同索引采样），并给出了相应的期望误差界和数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将矩阵中有效的自适应随机枢轴选择(ARP)方法扩展至张量t-乘积框架，解决张量交叉逼近中的索引选择问题。

Method: 提出两种构造：1) ARP-T-CUR：在傅里叶域对各 frontal 切片独立应用矩阵ARP，得到切片级CUR逼近并导出期望误差界；2) T-ARP：为整个张量选择共同的 lateral 和 horizontal 切片，在t-乘积意义下产生真正的张量交叉逼近，通过频率对齐条件建立期望误差界。

Result: T-ARP方法在频率对齐条件下恢复通常的r+1类型因子；数值实验表明共同索引张量采样优于标准张量交叉基线方法。

Conclusion: ARP方法可有效扩展至张量t-乘积框架，其中共同索引采样策略能产生更优的张量交叉逼近效果。

Abstract: This paper studies how adaptive randomized pivoting (ARP), recently introduced for matrix column subset selection, can be extended to tensors in the t-product framework. We propose two constructions. The first one, called ARP-T-CUR, applies matrix ARP independently to the frontal slices of the tensor in the Fourier domain. This gives a Fourier-slicewise CUR approximation and leads to a direct expected-error bound inherited from the matrix theory. The second construction, called T-ARP, selects common lateral and horizontal slices for the whole tensor. This produces a genuine tensor cross approximation in the t-product sense, but also introduces a new difficulty: the same pivot indices must be used across all Fourier slices. We make this coupling explicit and prove an expected-error bound under a frequency-alignment condition measuring how far the common tensor-level sampling rule is from the slice-wise ARP sampling rules. This condition recovers the usual $r+1$-type factor when the leverage-score distributions are aligned across frequencies. We also discuss the resulting tensor cross approximation and its connection with t-DEIM. Numerical experiments on synthetic tensors, images, and videos illustrate the behavior of the proposed methods and show the benefit of common-index tensor sampling over standard tensor cross baselines.

</details>


### [131] [TVD and TVB preservation without TVD time discretization for discontinuous Galerkin methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26692)
*Ziyao Xu,Zheng Sun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于迹限制的校正器框架，通过仅在最终阶段修改数值通量中的迹，将总变差稳定性控制与强稳定性保持（SSP）时间步长解耦，从而适用于RKDG、cRKDG和ADER-DG等多种不连续伽辽金格式，且不激活光滑区域的限制器以保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典RKDG框架中TVD/TVB性质依赖于SSP时间积分器，这带来了阶数限制且与cRKDG、ADER-DG等全离散格式不兼容。本文旨在解除这些约束，使得非SSP时间步长也能保证均值意义上的TVD/TVB性质。

Method: 基于Harten引理，本文观察到总变差稳定性仅由最终校正阶段的单元平均值更新决定。因此，仅在最终阶段修改数值通量中的迹（trace），而中间预测阶段不受约束。这一策略将振荡控制与SSP限制解耦，并兼容标准RKDG、cRKDG和ADER-DG预测器。

Result: 理论证明了该限制器在光滑区域不会激活，从而保持底层精度。数值实验展示了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该迹限制校正器框架成功突破了SSP时间步长的约束，为多种DG格式提供了灵活、高效且保持精度的振荡控制手段。

Abstract: Total variation diminishing (TVD) and total variation bounded (TVB) properties are crucial for controlling spurious oscillations in numerical solutions of conservation laws. In the classical Runge--Kutta (RK) discontinuous Galerkin (DG) framework, enforcing these properties is intrinsically tied to TVD time integrators, more commonly known today as strong-stability-preserving (SSP) methods. This reliance imposes severe structural restrictions, including order barriers and incompatibility with various fully discrete DG formulations, ranging from the recent RKDG method with compact stencils (cRKDG) to the widely established Arbitrary DERivative (ADER) DG method. To bypass these constraints, we propose a novel trace-limited corrector framework that preserves the TVD/TVB-in-the-means properties using generic, non-SSP time stepping. Based on Harten's lemma, our key insight is that total variation stability is dictated solely by the cell-average update in the final corrector stage. Consequently, we modify the traces in the numerical fluxes exclusively in the final stage, leaving the intermediate predictor stages unconstrained. This strategy decouples oscillation control from the SSP restriction, accommodates standard RKDG, cRKDG, and ADER-DG predictors, and retains the compactness of the cRKDG framework. We also prove that the limiter does not activate in smooth regions, thereby preserving the underlying accuracy. Finally, numerical experiments are presented to demonstrate the capabilities and robustness of the method.

</details>


### [132] [Regularization of the metric generalized inverse in Banach spaces and the dichotomy phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26730)
*Peter Mathé,Bernd Hofmann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Banach空间中非闭值域算子度量广义逆的迭代与参数正则化方法（Landweber、Schulz-Newton和Tikhonov），揭示了这些方法在应用于度量广义逆定义域内元素时能良好逼近最佳逼近解，而应用于定义域外元素时近似解将渐近无界这一二分性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人研究局限于闭值域情形，而现实问题中许多算子具有非闭值域（Nashed意义下的不适定问题），需要将度量广义逆的正则化理论推广到更一般的非闭值域情形。

Method: 定义了Landweber迭代、Schulz-Newton迭代和Tikhonov参数化正则化方法，并将其应用于非闭值域算子的度量广义逆近似；通过理论分析揭示近似解的二分性行为。

Result: 发现了这些正则化方案的基本二分性特征：当作用于度量广义逆定义域内的元素时，能很好地逼近最佳逼近解；当作用于定义域外的元素时，近似解会渐近无界发散。

Conclusion: 将度量广义逆的正则化理论从闭值域情形推广到非闭值域情形，揭示了迭代和参数正则化方法在非闭值域下的本质二分性，为Banach空间不适定问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: For a bounded linear operator acting between Banach spaces, its metric generalized inverse is the analog to the prominent Moore-Penrose inverse for operators acting between Hilbert spaces. This generalized inverse is well-defined for Banach spaces that are strictly convex and reflexive. Previous studies had been restricted to closed range situations, where the metric generalized inverse constitutes a continuous homogeneous mapping. The focus of the present study is on the ill-posed situation in the sense of Nashed, when the governing operator has a non-closed range. We define and analyze iterative schemes as the Landweber and the Schulz-Newton method, as well as parametric schemes with focus on a specific Tikhonov method. Both types are called regularizations aimed at approximating the metric generalized inverse. As a fundamental feature of such schemes we observe a dichotomy. This emphasizes that these schemes, when applied to elements of the domain of the metric generalized inverse, approximate the corresponding best approximate solutions well, whereas the resulting approximations will be asymptotically unbounded if they are applied to elements that do not belong to the domain of the metric generalized inverse.

</details>


### [133] [Recycling singular and projection subspaces for pseudospectra computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26785)
*Kuan Deng,Kuan Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子空间回收的框架，通过利用相邻网格点奇异值问题的相关性，加速大规模矩阵在指定区域上的伪谱计算，在保持精度的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在指定区域上计算矩阵伪谱需要评估大量网格点的最小奇异值，对于大规模矩阵计算成本过高，现有方法效率不足。

Method: 提出了基于子空间回收的加速框架：1) 利用相邻网格点奇异值问题的相关性，自适应回收先前计算点的奇异子空间；2) 开发了快速Rayleigh-Ritz-SVD程序从回收子空间中提取Ritz奇异对，并配合快速残差评估过程，整体计算成本与回收样本数呈线性关系；3) 对于可预条件的迭代SVD求解器，提出了两水平预条件子，其投影子空间可回收。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的回收策略相比现有方法实现了显著的加速，同时保持了伪谱计算的精度。

Conclusion: 该回收框架能有效加速稠密和稀疏矩阵的伪谱计算，在效率和精度之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Computing matrix pseudospectra over a prescribed region requires evaluating the smallest singular value of $C-zI$ at a large number of grid points, which can be prohibitively expensive for large-scale matrices. We develop a recycling-based framework for accelerating such computations for both dense and sparse matrices. The main idea is to exploit the correlation between singular value problems at neighboring grid points by adaptively recycling singular subspaces computed at previously visited points by an iterative SVD solver. We develop fast Rayleigh-Ritz-SVD procedures for extracting Ritz singular pairs from the recycled singular subspaces, together with fast residual evaluation procedures, with an overall cost that scales linearly with the number of recycled samples. When the iterative SVD solver admits preconditioning, we propose using a two-level preconditioner whose projection subspaces are recycled. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed recycling strategies yield substantial speedups over existing methods while maintaining the accuracy of the computed pseudospectra.

</details>


### [134] [Numerical analysis of first-order mean field games under displacement monotonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26853)
*Alpár R. Mészáros,Yohance A. P. Osborne*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于非可分位移单调Hamilton量的时变一阶平均场博弈系统数值近似的粒子方法，证明了在空间采样和时序离散联合极限下的收敛性及收敛速率，并通过数值实验验证了该方法在多种初始分布、时间跨度和空间维度下的有效性。这是首个处理非可分Hamilton量及奇异初始分布的长时域一阶MFG系统严格数值分析与近似的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值方法主要针对可分Hamilton量的MFG系统，而对非可分Hamilton量（尤其是位移单调类型）且可能具有奇异初始分布的长时间一阶MFG系统缺乏严格的数值近似理论与收敛性分析。

Method: 采用时间方向隐式Euler离散与空间采样的粒子方法，对连续MFG系统对应的Hamilton量/Pontryagin特征系统进行数值近似。分析中同时考虑空间样本数趋于无穷和时间步长趋于零的联合极限。

Result: 证明了玩家分布在$L^{\infty}(\mathcal{W}_2)$度量下的近似收敛性，以及沿最优轨迹的价值函数梯度在$L^{\infty}(L^2)$范数下的收敛性，并建立了收敛速率。在Lagrangian和终端成本额外满足局部Lipschitz连续时，还获得了价值函数在$L^{\infty}(L^1)$范数下的渐近误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个能处理非可分Hamilton量和奇异初始分布的长时域一阶MFG系统严格数值近似与分析的工作，数值实验验证了方法的广泛适用性。

Abstract: We introduce a particle method for the numerical approximation of time-dependent first-order Mean Field Games (MFGs) systems with non-separable, displacement monotone Hamiltonians and terminal costs, for arbitrary time-horizons and (possibly) singular initial player distributions in $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d)$. The numerical scheme is based on an implicit Euler discretization in time and sampling in space of the characteristic Hamiltonian/Pontryagin system associated with the continuous MFGs system. We prove convergence of the approximations of the player distribution in the $L^{\infty}(\mathcal{W}_2)$-metric and the approximations for the gradient of the value function along optimal trajectories in the $L^{\infty}{(L^2)}$-norm as the number of spatial samples tends to infinity jointly with the temporal time-step vanishing. The error bound that we establish for this convergence further implies rates of convergence of the scheme for a range of spatial sampling techniques. Provided that the Lagrangian and terminal costs are additionally locally Lipschitz continuous, we also establish an asymptotic error bound in the $L^{\infty}(L^1)$-norm for the approximations of the value function along optimal trajectories. This is the first work in the literature on rigorous numerical approximation and analysis of first-order MFG systems that handles non-separable Hamiltonians and potentially singular initial agent distributions for arbitrary long time horizons. We illustrate the performance of the scheme in numerical experiments for a range of initial agent distributions, time horizons and space dimensions.

</details>


### [135] [Accelerated sampling using SamAdams variable timesteps and position-adaptive Langevin dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26881)
*Benedict Leimkuhler,Peter A. Whalley*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合自适应时间步长（SamAdams）和位置自适应朗之万动力学（PAL）的加速采样方法（SA-PAL），在多种模型问题上相比固定步长集成提高了1.5倍至一个数量级以上的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有朗之万采样方法在刚体区域效率低下，需要更智能的自适应步长和摩擦张量策略来加速混合速率并保持精确不变测度。

Method: 结合两个互补机制：1) SamAdams自适应时间步长，通过松弛刚度监测自动缩短刚体区域的积分步长；2) 位置自适应朗之万（PAL）动力学，将摩擦集中在局部力方向同时保持正则分布为精确不变测度。采用回文型积分器实现，通过合理组织积分步骤和利用PAL摩擦张量的秩一加标量结构，每次迭代仅需一次力计算。

Result: 在Rosenbrock和Mueller-Brown势能上混合速率提升1.5-3倍；在薄熵通道和贝叶斯参数化问题中效率提升超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: SA-PAL方法在多种具有挑战性的模型问题上显著优于固定步长积分方案，尤其在高维或复杂势能面场景中具有较大应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce an accelerated Langevin-based sampling method that is based on two complementary devices: \emph{SamAdams} adaptive timestepping, which automatically shrinks the effective integration step in stiff regions of phase space using a relaxed stiffness monitor, and \emph{position-adaptive Langevin} (PAL) dynamics, which concentrates friction along the local force direction while preserving the canonical distribution as the exact invariant measure. The resulting combined scheme (SA-PAL) is implemented in a palindromic integrator which requires only one force evaluation per iteration through suitable organisation of the integration steps and by exploiting the rank-one-plus-scalar structure of the PAL friction tensor. We test the method on various model problems: the Rosenbrock function, a thin entropic channel, the Mueller-Brown potential, and a Bayesian parameterisation problem with a sparsity-inducing shrinkage prior. On the Rosenbrock and Mueller-Brown potentials mixing rates are improved by 1.5-3 times compared to fixed stepsize integration. Efficiency gains of more than an order of magnitude are documented in the other examples.

</details>


### [136] [Incremental Tensor-Train Compression from Streaming TT-Formatted Data: Applications to Reduced-Order Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26900)
*Wei Guo,Zhichao Peng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种确定性增量式张量列（TT）压缩算法，可直接处理流式TT格式数据，避免重建稠密张量，并建立了误差界，数值实验证明了其高效性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维张量数据流在科学和工程应用中普遍存在，现有流式TT算法通常需要从稠密张量数据或随机草图构建表示，但当数据直接以TT等低秩格式生成时，重建稠密张量既无必要且计算上不可行。

Method: 提出确定性增量TT压缩算法，通过核心张量维度投影、残差正交化和自适应扩充，在TT核层面直接操作，仅保留无法在给定容差内表示的互补信息，避免重建输入张量或累积全张量。

Result: 建立了增量方法的逼近误差界；证明了累积TT表示对应于全快照数据标准POD的压缩模拟；在参数化辐射传输方程上的数值实验表明，该方法在显著减少计算时间的同时达到可比的 reconstruction 精度，并能直接从压缩低秩数据构建高效准确的ROM。

Conclusion: 该算法可直接在TT核层面操作流式低秩数据，避免稠密重建，在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，为从流式压缩数据构建降阶模型提供了有效途径。

Abstract: High-dimensional tensor data streams arise naturally in scientific and engineering applications, such as simulations of kinetic equations and quantum systems, where samples become available sequentially and are often already represented in compressed low-rank tensor formats. Existing streaming tensor-train (TT) algorithms typically construct or update representations from dense tensor data or randomized sketches. However, when high-dimensional data are generated directly in TT or related low-rank formats, reconstructing dense tensors solely for the purpose of compression is unnecessary and computationally prohibitive. We develop a deterministic incremental TT compression algorithm that operates directly on streaming TT-formatted data. Given a new TT tensor, the proposed method updates an accumulated TT representation through core-wise projection, residual orthogonalization, and adaptive enrichment, retaining only the complementary information that cannot be represented within a prescribed tolerance. By operating entirely at the level of TT cores, the algorithm avoids reconstructing either the incoming tensor or the accumulated full tensor. We establish approximation error bounds for the proposed incremental approach. Moreover, we show that the accumulated TT representation corresponds to a compressed analogue of standard proper orthogonal decomposition for full-order snapshot data, enabling reduced-order models to be constructed directly from streaming low-rank solution data through operations on TT cores, without first reconstructing full snapshots. Numerical experiments on parametric radiative transfer equations demonstrate that the proposed method achieves comparable reconstruction accuracy with substantially reduced wall time and yields efficient and accurate ROMs directly from compressed low-rank data.

</details>


### [137] [Solving Shifted Systems for Quasiseparable Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26952)
*Luca Gemignani*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对半定拟可分离矩阵的移位线性系统快速数值算法，利用Givens旋转和双曲平面旋转更新Cholesky型分解，并通过生成元实现快速计算，数值实验验证了算法的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发针对半定拟可分离矩阵的移位线性系统的高效数值算法，解决此类特殊结构矩阵在移位后保持拟可分离性下的快速分解与求解问题。

Method: 结合Givens旋转和双曲平面旋转，更新拟可分离矩阵的Cholesky型分解，得到移位后矩阵的$LDL^T$分解（$L$为下三角，$D$为符号矩阵）；对于定号移位矩阵，利用正交变换稳定计算Cholesky分解；利用拟可分离性在移位下保持不变的特点，设计了基于生成元的快速更新变体。

Result: 数值实验表明所提算法具有有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的算法能够高效、稳定地求解半定拟可分离矩阵的移位线性系统，为相关数值计算问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper we develop fast numerical algorithms for solving shifted linear systems with semidefinite quasiseparable matrices.
  A combination of Givens and hyperbolic plane rotations is used to update the Cholesky-type factorization of the input quasiseparable matrix by
  determining a factorization of its shifted version of the form $LDL^T$, where $L$ is lower triangular and $D$ is a signature matrix.
  If the shifted matrix is also definite then the Cholesky factorization of the shifted matrix is computed in a stable way by using orthogonal transformations. Since quasiseparability is maintained under diagonal shifting, a fast variant of the updating procedure using
  computations with generators is also devised. Numerical experiments show the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [138] [An Arbitrary-Lagrangian-Eulerian solver for relativistic detonation waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27113)
*Sara Rinaldi,Olindo Zanotti,Michael Dumbser*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和数值模拟研究了相对论性爆震波的动力学行为，提出了一种包含反应项的总能量密度定义和反应物质量分数演化方程的方法，并开发了高效数值算法，成功模拟了洛伦兹因子高达γ_S~7的爆震波ZND剖面，发现了相对论性爆震波中Zeldovich压力跳变随质量通量先减后增的新效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对相对论性爆震波的动力学特性理解不足，尤其是相对论效应带来的新物理现象尚未被充分揭示，需要建立有效的理论框架和数值方法进行探索。

Method: 通过在总能量密度中引入额外项并增加反应物质量分数演化方程，保持质量与能量-动量守恒方程形式不变；采用二阶有限体积ALE格式（网格速度等于激波速度）和TVD重构；对刚性反应源项使用局部隐式算法；提出高效的守恒量到原始变量转换算法。

Result: 成功求解了洛伦兹因子γ_S~7的相对论性爆震波ZND剖面；发现了新的相对论效应：牛顿爆震中Zeldovich压力跳变随质量通量单调递减，而相对论爆震中压力跳变先达到最小值后单调上升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了相对论性爆震波中一个此前未被注意的新效应，该效应对从相对论爆震波中提取能量的物理过程具有潜在重要意义。

Abstract: In this paper we study the dynamics of relativistic detonation waves theoretically and numerically. The reaction is physically accounted for by an extra term in the definition of the total energy density and by an additional equation for the evolution of the mass fraction of the reactant, while leaving formally unmodified the equations of mass and energy-momentum conservation. In this way, the Rankine-Hugoniot relations maintain the same formal structure of the inert version. For the numerical solution we use a second order finite volume ALE scheme with TVD reconstruction, where the mesh velocity is chosen equal to the shock speed. We also adopt a locally implicit algorithm for the treatment of potentially stiff reaction source terms that arise in the equation of the reactant. We furthermore propose a particularly efficient algorithm for the conversion from the conserved to the primitive variables, which for the relativistic Euler equations is known to be nontrivial. Following this approach, we can successfully solve the Zel'dovich-von Neumann-Doering profile of a relativistsic detonation wave, up to Lorentz factors of the shock front $γ_S\sim 7$. Our analysis allowed us to highlight a new special relativistic effect, which has remained unnoticed so far. While in Newtonian detonations the Zel'dovich pressure jump decreases monotonically with the mass flux through the shock front, in the relativistic case it shows a minimum and then rises monotonically as a function of the mass flux. This may have interesting physical implications on the amount of energy that can be extracted from a relativistic detonation wave.

</details>


### [139] [Fast summation on rectangular cuboids with arbitrary periodicity in the DMK framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27134)
*David Krantz,Ludvig af Klinteberg,Anna-Karin Tornberg*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文扩展双空间多级核分裂（DMK）快速求和框架至矩形长方体区域，支持一、二或三维周期边界条件，通过傅里叶空间处理根层级远场贡献并利用截断核正则化奇异/近奇异傅里叶核，实现对所有周期性和高宽比的高效统一处理，且仅增加微小计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将原本适用于自由空间和立方体全周期问题的DMK快速求和框架扩展到矩形长方体区域，支持混合周期性边界条件（1D/2D/3D周期），填补现有方法在非立方体高宽比区域上的空白。

Method: 1）将周期化问题分解为局部化层级（子节点）和光滑根层级（远场）；2）局部层级在立方体平铺域上直接评估，仅需微小修改；3）根层级远场贡献在傅里叶空间中计算，周期方向用傅里叶级数，自由方向用傅里叶积分；4）对部分周期性问题，使用截断核正则化奇异/近奇异傅里叶核，保证梯形离散快速收敛；5）大宽高比时利用FFT加速根层级求和。

Result: 针对静电势、斯托克斯流（Stokeslet、Stresslet、Rotlet）势能，在所有周期性类型和宽高比下验证了方法的有效性。数值实验表明周期化处理仅对原始自由空间DMK算法增加少量额外开销，即使在高宽比矩形区域也是如此。

Conclusion: 该方法为混合周期性问题提供了统一的DMK应用框架，且可自然扩展到其他具有核分裂形式的非振荡核函数。

Abstract: Dual-space multilevel kernel-splitting (DMK) is a fast summation framework that combines ideas from the fast multipole method, Ewald summation, and multilevel summation. Originally formulated for free-space problems, and later extended to fully periodic problems on a cube, it decomposes the kernel interaction into a smooth global contribution and a hierarchy of localized interactions evaluated on an octree. We extend DMK to problems on rectangular cuboids with periodic boundary conditions in one, two, or three coordinate directions. The periodization leverages the fact that interactions on all tree levels below the root are localized, allowing for their evaluation with minimal modification on a cubical tiling of the domain. The remaining smooth root-level far-field contribution is evaluated in Fourier space, with Fourier series in the periodic directions and Fourier integrals in the free directions. For reduced periodicity, truncated kernels are used to regularize singular and near-singular Fourier kernels, yielding rapidly convergent trapezoidal discretizations and a unified treatment of all periodicities. For large-aspect-ratio cuboids, the root-level sum can be accelerated using the fast Fourier transform. We validate the method for the electrostatic potential and Stokeslet, stresslet and rotlet potentials, for all periodicities and a wide range of aspect ratios. Numerical experiments show that the periodization adds only a small overhead to the original free-space DMK algorithm, also for high-aspect-ratio cuboids. The resulting method provides a framework for applying DMK to problems with mixed periodicity on rectangular cuboids, and extends naturally to other non-oscillatory kernels for which a kernel split is available.

</details>


### [140] [On Fourier Phase Retrieval from Differential Intensity Measurements with Applications to Wavefront Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27176)
*Simon Hubmer,Lukas Weissinger,Ronny Ramlau,Otmar Scherzer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究从微分强度测量中进行傅里叶相位恢复，通过推广光强传输方程原理，将相位重建转化为求解偏微分或积分-微分方程，并应用于金字塔波前传感器等自适应光学系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决傅里叶域中从微分强度测量恢复相位的问题，为金字塔波前传感器等自适应光学器件提供高效的重建算法。

Method: 推广光强传输方程原理，将相位恢复问题转化为求解偏微分方程或积分-微分方程。

Result: 设计了针对傅里叶型波前传感器（如金字塔波前传感器）的高效重建算法，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 微分强度测量可经由偏微分/积分-微分方程实现相位恢复，为自适应光学中的波前传感提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider Fourier phase retrieval from differential intensity measurements, i.e., the problem of determining the phase of a complex-valued function from a series of intensity measurements differing only by slight modulations in Fourier domain. These modulations may be induced by optical elements such as prisms or phase plates, or via spatial-light modulators. Generalizing the principles behind the transport of intensity equation, we show that given such differential intensity measurements, the phase can be determined as the solution of certain partial differential or integro-differential equations. This is then used to design efficient reconstruction algorithms for a number of Fourier-type wavefront sensors, such as the pyramid wavefront sensor, commonly used in adaptive optics. Numerical experiments illustrate the usefulness of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [141] [De la Vallée Poussin type approximation for solving some Fredholm integral equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27178)
*Domenico Mezzanotte,Donatella Occorsio,Mario Pezzella,Woula Themistoclakis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于de la Vallée Poussin型多项式逼近（在Jacobi零点处）的数值方法，用于求解第二类Fredholm积分方程。该方法在加权函数空间中具有一致有界的Lebesgue常数、近最优一致逼近能力，并能有效缓解Gibbs现象，尤其适用于处理端点奇异性和弱奇异/高振荡核函数。理论证明了加权一致空间下的稳定性与收敛性，数值实验验证了其优于传统Lagrange投影方法的局部精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Lagrange插值在求解Fredholm积分方程时存在Lebesgue常数无界、Gibbs现象严重等问题，且难以有效处理函数端点奇异性和核函数的弱奇异或高振荡行为。因此需要一种更稳定、高精度的数值方法。

Method: 基于de la Vallée Poussin型多项式逼近，在Jacobi零点处构造近似，通过求解良态线性系统实现，并在加权一致空间中分析稳定性和收敛性。

Result: Lebesgue常数在加权函数空间中一致有界；实现了近最优一致逼近；有效缓解了Gibbs现象；方法在加权一致空间中稳定且收敛；数值实验证实了理论误差估计，且局部精度优于Lagrange投影法。

Conclusion: 基于de la Vallée Poussin型多项式逼近的方法是求解第二类Fredholm积分方程的一种有效且高精度的数值方案，尤其适用于具有奇异性和振荡性的问题。

Abstract: In the present paper, we introduce a numerical method for second-kind Fredholm integral equations (FIEs) based on de la Vallée Poussin-type (VP) polynomial approximations at Jacobi zeros. This class of approximations offers several advantages over classical Lagrange interpolation at the same nodes. In particular, it guarantees uniformly bounded Lebesgue constants in suitable weighted function spaces and provides near-best uniform approximation for functions in these spaces, while also significantly mitigating the Gibbs phenomenon. We show how these properties can be exploited in the numerical solution of FIEs. In particular, the proposed approach effectively handles functions with possible algebraic endpoint singularities and kernel functions featuring weak singularities or highly oscillatory behavior. Under suitable assumptions, we prove stability and convergence of the method in weighted uniform spaces. Furthermore, we develop an efficient implementation based on the solution of a well-conditioned linear system. Numerical results confirm the theoretical error estimates and show that the proposed method achieves higher local accuracy than the corresponding Lagrange-based projection method.

</details>


### [142] [Hierarchical Muon: Tiled Newton-Schulz Updates for Efficient Muon Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27216)
*Ziyuan Tang,Tianshi Xu,Yousef Saad,Yuanzhe Xi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: HiMuon将Muon型优化器中的全矩阵Newton-Schulz更新分解为独立的分块操作，在保持训练行为接近全矩阵版本的同时显著降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon型优化器对稠密权重矩阵的全矩阵Newton-Schulz更新计算开销大（$O(r^2 s K)$），需要解耦所有行列的耦合以提升效率。

Method: 将动量梯度矩阵划分为$T \times T$个分块，对每个分块独立施加相同的有限Newton-Schulz映射，再重组结果。这定义了局部矩阵函数映射，块内保留谱交互，块间丢弃。

Result: 计算复杂度降至$O(H W T K)$，支持GPU分块核函数、跨层批处理、内存受限分块和运行时块大小调度。在Transformer训练和矩阵函数诊断实验中，HiMuon提升了优化器步效率，且训练行为接近全矩阵Muon。

Conclusion: HiMuon通过分块策略有效降低了Muon型优化器的计算开销，同时在测试范围内保持了与全矩阵版本相近的训练性能。

Abstract: Muon-type optimizers construct update directions for dense neural-network weights by applying a finite Newton-Schulz map to momentum-gradient matrices. For an $H \times W$ matrix, with $r=\min\{H,W\}$ and $s=\max\{H,W\}$, $K$ steps of the full-matrix Newton-Schulz update require $O(r^2 s K)$ work and couple all rows and columns through repeated Gram matrix products. We introduce Hierarchical Muon (HiMuon), a tiled Newton-Schulz scheme for Muon-type optimization. HiMuon partitions each momentum-gradient matrix into $T \times T$ tiles, applies the same finite Newton-Schulz map independently to each tile, and reassembles the results. For finite $T$ below the matrix dimensions, HiMuon defines a local matrix-function map rather than a convergent approximation to the full-matrix update: spectral interactions are preserved within tiles and discarded across tile boundaries. For fixed finite $T$, the leading Newton-Schulz work decreases to $O(H W T K)$, and the computation decomposes into independent small dense matrix operations. This structure enables tile-size-dependent GPU kernels, cross-layer batching, memory-bounded chunking, and runtime tile-size schedules. Experiments on transformer training and controlled matrix-function diagnostics show that HiMuon improves optimizer-step efficiency while keeping training behavior close to full-matrix Muon in the tested regimes.

</details>


### [143] [Quasi-Feynman formulas that provide fast converging Chernoff approximations to solution of parabolic differential equation on the real line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27232)
*Ivan D. Remizov,Alexandr V. Vedenin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Chernoff乘积公式的新型拟Feynman公式，用于显式逼近实线上变系数二阶抛物型偏微分方程的解，通过有界区间上的Riemann积分构造算子值函数，实现了空间-时间一致收敛的二次速率，优于传统的一阶估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Chernoff乘积公式的标准一阶收敛率，为变系数抛物型方程提供一种既具有严格误差界又便于数值实现的显式逼近方法。

Method: 基于Chernoff乘积公式，通过有界区间上的Riemann积分构造新的算子值函数，利用半群理论和多重有界积分技术，推导出非网格、非Galerkin类型的拟Feynman公式。

Result: 对于充分光滑的初值和系数，证明了Chernoff逼近在空间和时间上一致收敛，且收敛阶为二次，显著改进了标准的一阶估计。通过符号计算验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅提供了更精确的误差界，还为抛物型问题提供了一种实用的解析工具，所提出的拟Feynman公式类兼具理论严谨性和数值实用性。

Abstract: We construct explicit approximations to the solution of a second-order parabolic partial differential equation on the real line with variable coefficients. The method is based on Chernoff's product formula and uses a new operator-valued function defined through proper Riemann integrals over a bounded interval, which makes the approach readily usable in numerical practice. For sufficiently smooth initial data and coefficients, we prove that the resulting Chernoff approximations converge uniformly in space and time with a quadratic rate, improving the standard first-order estimate. The construction yields a new class of quasi-Feynman formulas that are neither grid-based nor Galerkin-type, but instead rely on semigroup theory and multiple bounded integrals. The theoretical findings are validated by symbolic computation, and the paper contributes both refined error bounds and a practical analytical tool for parabolic problems.

</details>


### [144] [Adaptive Iterative Numerical Homogenization for Quasilinear Nonmonotone Elliptic PDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27256)
*Maher Khrais,Barbara Verfürth*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种自适应迭代数值均匀化方法，用于求解一类多尺度拟线性非单调椭圆问题。该方法基于局部正交分解（LOD）技术，结合Kačanov迭代，通过自适应更新多尺度基函数和可计算的误差指示器，实现了高效求解，并提供了先验误差估计和收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多尺度拟线性非单调椭圆问题在数值求解中面临的挑战，特别是非线性与多尺度耦合导致的计算复杂性问题，需要一种能够自适应调整计算资源的高效数值方法。

Method: 基于局部正交分解（LOD）技术，在Kačanov迭代的每一步中自适应地重新计算多尺度基函数并求解线性问题。核心创新是提出了一个局部可计算的误差指示器，在每个迭代步中识别需要更新的基函数，同时尽可能保留先前已计算的基函数。此外还讨论了该方法在牛顿迭代框架下的适配。

Result: 提供了先验误差估计，证明了方法的收敛性，仅要求右端项具有更高的可积性，而不要求解本身具有更高的可微性。数值实验验证了理论结果和方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应迭代数值均匀化方法能有效处理多尺度拟线性非单调椭圆问题，通过自适应基函数更新策略在保持精度的同时降低计算成本，具有理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We propose and analyze an adaptive iterative numerical homogenization method to approximate the solution of a class of quasilinear nonmonotone elliptic problems that is of multiscale nature. The method is based on the technique of the Localized Orthogonal Decomposition (LOD) applied to the linear problems in each step of a Kačanov iteration. In this approach, the multiscale basis is recomputed adaptively in each iteration and a linear problem is solved with this updated multiscale space, where in both steps the nonlinearity is evaluated using the approximation of the previous iteration. As a key component of the proposed approach, we present a locally computable error indicator, which at each iteration identifies the basis functions requiring updates, while previously computed basis functions are retained whenever possible. We provide a priori error estimates and show convergence of the method, requiring only higher integrability of the right-hand side, but no higher differentiability of the solution itself. Furthermore, we discuss how to adapt the proposed adaptive iterative LOD in the context of Newton's method as iteration scheme. Numerical experiments illustrate the theory and validate the applicability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [145] [A modified Euler-Maruyama method to simulate a one-dimensional sticky diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27259)
*Chenqi Jiang,Miranda Holmes-Cerfon*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数值算法，用于模拟可粘附/脱离一维点的粘性扩散过程，该算法通过对标准Euler-Maruyama方案进行简单修改实现，并证明了其以1阶弱收敛到目标粘性扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性扩散过程可以粘附到低维边界并从中脱离，模拟这类过程的关键挑战在于以动态一致的方式捕捉维度的变化。

Method: 对标准Euler-Maruyama方案进行简单修改：以一定概率在反射Euler-Maruyama更新与跳跃到粘性点之间进行选择。该概率的选择与期望动力学的生成元一致。

Result: 证明了该方案以1阶弱收敛到粘性扩散过程。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法能有效模拟一维粘性扩散过程，且具有明确的收敛阶保证。

Abstract: A sticky diffusion is a process that can stick to and detach from a lower-dimensional boundary. A challenge in simulating such a process is in capturing the change in dimension in a dynamically consistent way. We introduce a numerical algorithm to simulate a one-dimensional sticky diffusion, which sticks to and detaches from a point. Our method is a simple modification of the standard Euler-Maruyama scheme, which chooses with some probability between a reflected Euler-Maruyama update and a jump to the sticky point. We show how to choose this probability to be consistent with the generator of the desired dynamics, and we prove that our scheme converges weakly to a sticky diffusion with order 1.

</details>


### [146] [Automated Galerkin time stepping in Irksome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27300)
*Boris D. Andrews,Pablo Brubeck,Patrick E. Farrell,Robert C. Kirby,Scott P. MacLachlan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Irksome中自动化的Galerkin时间离散格式（dG和cPG），支持辅助变量、灵活时间求积和整体代数求解器，用户可通过最小代码修改在Runge-Kutta和Galerkin格式间切换，数值实验验证了精度、求解器性能和结构保持特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galerkin时间离散格式具有比Runge-Kutta更优越的结构保持特性，但其实现一直依赖于特制代码，限制了广泛使用。本文旨在通过Irksome框架实现这些格式的自动化，降低使用门槛。

Method: 在Irksome中实现了不连续Galerkin (dG) 和连续Petrov-Galerkin (cPG) 时间步进格式的自动化，支持辅助变量、灵活的时间求积策略和整体代数求解器，并允许用户在最小代码改动下切换Runge-Kutta与Galerkin格式。

Result: 数值实验展示了该方法在代表性PDE系统上的精度、求解器性能及结构保持特性，验证了自动化的Galerkin时间离散方案的有效性。

Conclusion: Irksome的自动化实现使得Galerkin时间离散格式不再依赖特制代码，大幅提升了其可用性，为更广泛的结构保持数值模拟提供了有效工具。

Abstract: As the study of temporal and spatial discretization schemes continues to advance, recent work has focused on the use of Galerkin-in-time discretization schemes that enable broader structure-preservation than is known for Runge-Kutta integrators. While the promise of such discretizations is immense, their realization has, until now, generally relied on bespoke implementations that have limited their wider use. In this work, we present automation in Irksome for both discontinuous Galerkin and continuous Petrov-Galerkin time stepping of semidiscrete variational problems. The implementation supports auxiliary variables, flexible temporal quadrature, and monolithic algebraic solvers, and it enables switching between Runge-Kutta and Galerkin-in-time formulations with minimal changes to user code. Numerical examples illustrate accuracy, solver performance, and structure preservation across representative PDE systems.

</details>

### [147] [Higher-Order vs. Quadratic Binary Optimization: Which Is Better for Probability Optimization with Tensor Sampling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26206)
*Hadi Salloum,Kirill Novoselov,Aleksandr Pochtarev,Yaroslav Kholodov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文比较了高阶无约束二元优化（HUBO）和二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）模型在概率优化中的表现，发现HUBO在包含高阶交互的原始问题上优势明显，而QUBO在天然二次问题上仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为概率优化任务中HUBO与QUBO模型的选择提供理论分析和实验指导，探索两者在表达力、降阶开销和求解器性能方面的权衡。

Method: 结合理论分析（形式化表达能力与降阶开销）和实证实验（合成多项式目标、RSA因子分解、Max-Cut实例），比较HUBO与QUBO在张量采样技术下的性能。

Result: HUBO在原始问题包含高阶交互时更优，因为将其降阶为QUBO会增加张量采样器面临的有效维度；QUBO在天然二次问题上仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 为选择优化框架提供了实用指导，需在模型保真度、维度增长和求解器性能之间进行权衡。

Abstract: This article explores the comparative strengths of Higher-Order Unconstrained Binary Optimization (HUBO) and Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) models in the context of probability optimization using tensor sampling techniques. HUBO can represent interactions beyond pairwise terms without introducing auxiliary variables, while QUBO benefits from a simpler algebraic structure and a wide range of established solvers. We combine a theoretical analysis of formulation expressiveness and reduction overhead with empirical experiments on synthetic polynomial objectives, RSA factorization, and Max-Cut instances. The results show that native HUBO formulations are often preferable when the original problem contains high-order interactions, because reducing such problems to QUBO increases the effective dimension seen by tensor samplers. At the same time, QUBO remains competitive for naturally quadratic problems. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting an optimization framework that balances model fidelity, dimension growth, and solver performance.

</details>


### [148] [Three-Objective Integral R2 Subset Selection: NP-Hardness and Submodular Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26591)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了三目标优化中基于积分R2指标的固定基数代表点子集选择问题。理论上证明了该指标的R2改进量是单调子模集函数（正结果），同时证明了精确固定基数子集选择是NP-hard（负结果），从而将可处理的两目标情形与三目标情形区分开，并给出了基于子模优化的近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限帕累托前沿近似中选择固定数量的代表点是多目标优化的基本后处理任务。本文旨在研究三目标情形下基于积分R2指标的代表点选择问题，特别关注其计算复杂性和近似可行性。

Method: 正面：证明积分R2改进量（相对于固定基线）是单调子模集函数，从而贪心选择能保证至少$(1-1/e)$的最优间隙缩减；给出通过细分计算精确积分R2值的贪心实现，最坏时间复杂度$O(n^6)$。负面：通过透视变换将Tchebycheff阴影改进映射到加权锚定框并集问题（密度$(x_1+x_2+x_3)^{-4}$），再适配Bringmann等人的三维锚定框构造，证明精确固定基数子集选择是NP-hard。

Result: 正面：贪心选择能关闭至少$(1-1/e)$的最大可能R2间隙；实现了$O(n^6)$的贪心算法。负面：三目标精确子集选择是NP-hard，与可多项式求解的两目标情形形成对比。

Conclusion: 三目标情形在精确求解上是NP-hard，但通过子模优化可提供可证明的近似保证，为实际应用提供了理论支撑的近似路线。

Abstract: Selecting a fixed number of representative points from a finite Pareto-front approximation is a fundamental post-processing task in multiobjective optimization. This paper studies this problem for the integral R2 indicator in three objectives, where the indicator is defined as the integral of the lower envelope of weighted Tchebycheff scalarizations over the two-dimensional weight simplex. We provide two complementary algorithmic results. On the positive side, we show that the integral R2 improvement with respect to any fixed baseline is a monotone submodular set function. For the usual ideal-point based R2 indicator, with the ideal point fixed, this yields a direct gap-reduction guarantee: greedy selection closes at least a $(1-1/e)$-fraction of the maximum possible R2 gap between a fixed dominated anchor value and the best cardinality-$k$ value. We also give a tested greedy implementation that evaluates exact integral R2 values by subdivision, with worst-case running time $O(n^6)$. On the negative side, we prove that exact fixed-cardinality subset selection is NP-hard already in three objectives. The hardness proof uses a perspective transformation that maps Tchebycheff-shadow improvements to a weighted anchored-box union problem with density $(x_1+x_2+x_3)^{-4}$, and then adapts the three-dimensional anchored-box construction of Bringmann, Cabello, and Emmerich. Together, these results separate the tractable two-objective case from the three-objective case while identifying a principled approximation route based on submodular optimization.

</details>


### [149] [Input Convex Neural Network as a Surrogate in Stability-Constrained Optimization for IBR-dominated Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26446)
*Wangkun Xu,Hongyang Jia,Yi Wang,Ning Zhang,Fei Teng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文澄清了输入凸神经网络（ICNN）在电力系统优化中作为替代模型时的两个常见公式化局限性：不必要的Big-M混合整数重构和不等式反转导致的非凸性，并提出了相应的精确LP重构、外逼近和内逼近方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICNN作为电力系统稳定性指标的替代模型时，若公式化处理不当会抵消其凸性优势，因此需要明确这些局限性并提供正确的处理方法。

Method: (i) 针对ReLU-ICNN提出基于LP的精确epigraph重构；(ii) 在子水平集约定下提出具有全局保证的外逼近方案；(iii) 在超水平集约定下提出保持可行性的内逼近方案。

Result: 在IEEE 14节点和118节点机组组合实例上进行了仿真验证。

Conclusion: 正确使用ICNN凸性优势需要避免常见的公式化误区，本文提出的三种方案可有效解决这些问题。

Abstract: Input convex neural networks (ICNNs) are increasingly used as surrogates for stability indices and embedded as constraints in power-system optimization. This letter clarifies two recurring formulation limitations that can negate ICNN convexity benefits: (i) applying generic Big-$M$ mixed-integer reformulations introduces auxiliary binaries that are unnecessary for enforcing ICNN sublevel constraints; and (ii) reversing the stability inequality transforms a convex sublevel set into a generally nonconvex superlevel set, invalidating global-convergence guarantees of cut-based methods. After clarifying the limitations, we provide (i) an exact LP-based epigraph reformulation for ReLU-ICNNs, (ii) an outer-approximation scheme with global guarantees under the sublevel convention, and (iii) a feasibility-preserving inner-approximation scheme for the superlevel convention, with simulations on IEEE 14- and 118-bus unit commitment instances.

</details>


### [150] [Minkowski-Type Wasserstein Metrics and Barycenters for Location-Scale Mixtures with Application to Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26482)
*Songyan Luo,Yunxin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对有限位置-尺度混合模型的离散最优传输框架，通过将连续多边缘OT问题简化为混合组件间的离散传输问题，使计算复杂度从样本量的二次方降至线性，并在VisDA-C基准上验证了其竞争性精度与显著降低的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散最优传输依赖于经验度量间的逐点匹配，计算成本随样本量至少呈二次增长，限制了其在大规模数据中的应用。本文旨在通过利用混合模型的结构特性来克服这一计算瓶颈。

Method: 基于广义Minkowski不等式定义特定函数类，刻画多变量位置-尺度族之间的OT映射；利用可辨识性假设将Wasserstein型度量和重心扩展到混合模型；通过将联合耦合约束为特定的混合结构，将连续多边缘OT问题转化为混合组件间的离散传输问题。

Result: 在VisDA-C基准上的实证评估表明，该方法在达到与现有经验OT方法竞争性精度的同时，显著降低了计算成本和内存占用，计算复杂度从二次降至线性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效解决了离散OT计算复杂度随样本量二次增长的问题，为大规模最优传输提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Discrete optimal transport (OT) typically relies on pointwise matching between empirical measures, incurring computational costs that scale at least quadratically with the sample size. To circumvent this limitation, we introduce a mathematical framework for OT between finite location-scale mixture models. By defining a specific function class grounded in generalized Minkowski inequalities and characterizing OT maps between multivariate location-scale families, we extend Wasserstein-type metrics and barycenters to these mixture models under the assumption of identifiability. Furthermore, we prove that restricting joint couplings to a specific mixture structure reduces the continuous multimarginal OT problem to a discrete transport problem over mixture components. Computing transport plans between these components rather than individual samples reduces the computational complexity to linear scaling with respect to the sample size. Empirical evaluations on the VisDA-C benchmark confirm that this strategy achieves competitive accuracy compared to existing empirical OT approaches, while substantially reducing the computational cost and memory footprint.

</details>


### [151] [Mean-Field PhiBE: Continuous-Time Mean-Field Reinforcement Learning from Discrete-Time Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26498)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Martin Hernandez,Qinxin Yan,Yuhua Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间平均场控制问题，提出了一种基于离散时间数据的无模型方法。通过引入Mean-Field-PhiBE (MF-PhiBE)，将离散时间转移信息融入Wasserstein空间上的连续时间PDE，避免了经典方法中漂移和扩散系数不可辨识的问题。结合策略梯度定理，构建了无模型演员-评论家方法，并证明了策略值误差为O(Δt)的一阶一致性估计，在线性二次情形下可达二阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间平均场控制中，策略评估需要求解Wasserstein空间上的平稳HJB方程，但该方程涉及McKean-Vlasov动力学的漂移和扩散系数，在仅能获取离散时间转移数据时这些系数不可辨识。直接简化为时间离散Bellman方程虽避免了不可辨识问题，但丢失了微分方程结构。因此需要一种方法：既能利用离散时间数据，又能保留连续时间PDE的生成元结构。

Method: 1. 提出Mean-Field-PhiBE (MF-PhiBE)，将离散时间一步转移数据中的经验估计量替换HJB方程中未知的无穷小漂移与协方差项，同时保留McKean-Vlasov HJB方程的生成元结构。2. 推导熵正则化随机反馈策略的策略梯度定理，通过动作方向的无穷小优势函数与策略得分表达演员更新方向。3. 基于上述两个核心组件构建无模型演员-评论家方法。

Result: 1. 证明了最优MF-PhiBE策略对应的值与最优连续时间值之间的误差为O(Δt)的一阶一致性估计。2. 在线性二次情形下，仅需一步数据即可达到二阶精度。3. 通过LQR基准测试和人群厌恶问题的数值实验验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: MF-PhiBE有效地弥合了离散时间数据和连续时间PDE之间的鸿沟，在仅使用离散时间转移数据的情况下，实现了对连续时间平均场控制问题的无模型求解，且理论上保证了逼近精度。

Abstract: This paper addresses model-free continuous-time mean-field control in a setting where the population dynamics evolve continuously according to an unknown McKean-Vlasov stochastic differential equation, while only discrete-time transition data are available. In the model-based formulation, policy evaluation is naturally described by a stationary Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation on $\mathcal P_2(\mathbb R^d)$, but this equation involves the drift and diffusion coefficients of the controlled McKean-Vlasov dynamics, which are not identifiable when only discrete-time data are available. On the other hand, a direct reduction to a time-discrete Bellman equation avoids the non-identifiability issue but loses the differential equation structure. To bridge these two viewpoints, we introduce a Mean-Field-PhiBE (MF-PhiBE), which incorporates discrete-time transition information into a continuous-time PDE on the Wasserstein space. The MF-PhiBE replaces the unknown infinitesimal drift and covariance in the policy-evaluation equation by one-step estimators computed from data, while preserving the generator structure of the McKean-Vlasov HJB equation. We also derive a policy-gradient theorem for entropy-regularized randomized feedback policies, expressing the actor direction through an action-wise infinitesimal advantage and the score of the policy. Combining these two ingredients yields a model-free actor-critic method. We prove a first-order consistency estimate showing that the value induced by an optimal MF-PhiBE policy approximates the optimal continuous-time value with an error of order $Δt$. In the linear-quadratic case, we show our approximation achieves second-order accuracy with only one-step data. Numerical experiments on an LQR benchmark and a crowd-aversion problem illustrate the proposed framework.

</details>


### [152] [Maximizing Throughput in an M/G/1 Queue with Customer Abandonments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26544)
*Runhua Wu,Hayriye Ayhan,Douglas G. Down*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有顾客放弃的单服务器队列中的最优服务器分配策略，证明当服务时间服从Erlang-K或超指数分布且决策者可观察服务阶段时，最短剩余期望处理时间（SREPT）策略能最大化长期平均吞吐量，且该结果与放弃率无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在顾客可能放弃（不耐烦）的排队系统中，如何选择最优的服务分配策略以最大化系统的长期平均吞吐量。

Method: 考虑具有泊松到达和指数分布耐心时间的单服务器队列系统，假设服务时间服从Erlang-K分布或超指数分布，且决策者能够观察顾客服务时间的阶段信息，分析SREPT策略的最优性。

Result: 证明了SREPT策略在这些设定下能最大化长期平均吞吐量，且该最优性独立于顾客的放弃率。

Conclusion: 在具有顾客放弃的排队系统中，SREPT策略是一种鲁棒的最优服务分配策略，其最优性不受放弃率影响。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of identifying the optimal server assignment policy in single-server queues with customer abandonment. We consider a system with Poisson arrivals and exponentially distributed patience times. We show that when service times follow either an Erlang-$K$ or a hyperexponential distribution and the decision maker can observe the phase of a customer's service time, the Shortest Remaining Expected Processing Time (SREPT) policy maximizes the long-run average throughput, independent of the abandonment rate.

</details>


### [153] [Comparative Analysis of Linear Battery Models for Carbon Emission Optimization in Solar Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26648)
*Anna Sacilotto,Ambrogio Maria Bernardelli,Stefano Gualandi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对住宅光伏-电池储能系统（PV-BESS）在不确定性下的碳排放最小化问题，建立并比较了从规则启发式到完全随机规划的一系列线性优化模型。实验表明，直接随机优化预期排放（可编程电池模型）显著优于启发式控制，接近理论最优下界；带反馈的模型在训练集上略有提升但泛化性差且计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有住宅光伏-电池储能系统在不确定性条件下的碳排放最小化问题缺乏系统性比较研究，特别是不同决策框架（从启发式到随机优化）在碳排放目标下的性能差异尚不明确。

Method: 构建了从规则型自发自用启发式到完全随机规划（含线性反馈控制）的层级线性优化模型体系；通过主成分分析处理真实运行数据生成大规模场景集，显式建模家庭负荷、光伏发电和电网碳强度的随机变化。

Result: 可编程电池模型的碳排放削减效果接近全能型电池理论下界；带反馈的模型在训练集上表现略优但无法泛化到未见数据，且计算开销显著更高。

Conclusion: 线性随机规划为分布式光伏-电池储能系统提供了既有效又易处理的碳排放感知能源管理框架。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of minimizing equivalent carbon emissions in residential photovoltaic-battery energy storage systems (PV-BESS) under uncertainty. We develop and compare a hierarchy of linear optimization models that differ in their degree of anticipativity and feedback complexity, ranging from a rule-based self-consumption heuristic to fully stochastic formulations with linear feedback control. The proposed models explicitly incorporate the stochastic variability of household load, solar production, and grid carbon intensity through large scenario sets generated via principal component analysis of real operational data. Computational experiments on synthetic yet realistic scenarios show that direct stochastic optimization of expected emissions (Programmed Battery model) substantially outperforms heuristic control, achieving emission reductions close to the theoretical lower bound provided by the Omniscient Battery benchmark. Feedback-based models marginally improve training performance but do not generalize better on unseen data, while incurring higher computational costs. Overall, results demonstrate that linear stochastic programming provides an effective and tractable framework for emission-aware energy management in distributed PV-BESS systems.

</details>


### [154] [The parental parsimony problem on binary, tree-child phylogenetic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26919)
*Martin Frohn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了系统发育网络中亲本简约得分问题（PPS），首次提出了常数因子近似算法和精确求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育重建是计算生物学的主要挑战之一，在系统发育网络中寻找最简约树是重要子任务。现有变体对应于不同的生物学模型假设（杂交、重组、异源多倍体、不完全谱系分选），而PPS问题作为NP难组合优化问题，缺乏有效的近似算法和精确求解方法。

Method: 对于任意但固定的叶子标签，提出了PPS问题的首个常数因子近似算法；针对一类保持NP难的网络（二叉树-孩子系统发育网络），设计了一种新的精确求解算法，并在模拟数据上分析其性能。

Result: 首次给出了PPS问题的常数因子近似算法，并提出了针对二叉树-孩子系统发育网络的精确求解算法，在模拟数据上进行了性能评估。

Conclusion: 该工作为NP难的PPS问题提供了首个近似保证，同时提出的精确算法为系统发育网络中的最简约树查找提供了新的求解工具。

Abstract: Phylogenetic reconstruction is one of the major challenges in computational biology. Among existing reconstruction methods for phylogenetic networks, an important subtask emerges in extending a leaf-labelling on a phylogenetic network to determine a most parsimonious tree inside the network. There exist different variants of this subtask depending on the biological model assumptions for which distinct evolutionary phenomena are captured by the network. In this article we assume that next to hybridization or recombination events, also allopolyploidy or incomplete lineage sorting are present. Then, finding the most parsimonious tree inside the network is called the parental parsimony score problem (PPS), a NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem. We provide the first constant-factor approximation for the PPS on arbitrary but fixed leaf labels and a class of networks on which the PPS remains NP-hard, namely binary, semi-simplex, tree-child phylogenetic networks. Furthermore, we introduce a novel exact solution algorithm for the PPS on binary, tree-child phylogenetic networks and analyze its performance on simulated data.

</details>


### [155] [Sinkhorn-Knopp balancing with generalised martingale-type constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26739)
*Abigail Langbridge,Martin Corless,Robert Shorten*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于熵正则化最优传输的推广框架，通过类Sinkhorn算法求解具有一般运输约束和异质性柔性边际约束的资源分配问题，并证明了算法的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享经济等实际场景中，资源分配问题不仅涉及运输计划，还需考虑其具体实现，且常包含一般运输约束和异质性柔性边际约束。经典的最优传输模型难以处理这些复杂约束，因此需要更通用的框架。

Method: 将经典的熵正则化最优传输问题进行推广，以纳入一般运输约束和异质性柔性边际约束，并设计了可证明收敛的类Sinkhorn算法来求解该问题。

Result: 提出了收敛性得到证明的Sinkhorn类算法，并通过示例展示了方法的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该推广框架能够有效处理具有一般约束和异质性柔性边际的资源分配问题，收敛性有理论保证，在共享经济等应用中有重要价值。

Abstract: We consider the problem of optimally distributing resources from a set of suppliers to a set of consumers in the presence of general transportation constraints, and including heterogeneous flexibility in the marginal constraints. Such problems frequently arise in a variety of practical settings; for example, in the context of sharing economy applications, where one is not only interested in the transportation plan, but also its realisation, and in other problems that involve the study of martingales. Our principal contribution in this paper is to consider a generalisation of the classic entropically regularised Optimal Transport formulation in which such problems can be solved with a Sinkhorn algorithm. In particular, we present provably convergent Sinkhorn-like algorithms for solving this class of problems, and provide examples to both illustrate the utility of our approach as well as its efficacy.

</details>


### [156] [Second-order Methods for Multiobjective Composite Optimization: Preconditioning Strategies, Subspace Variants and Inexact Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26792)
*Jian Chen,Xinmin Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对多目标复合优化问题中光滑分量病态条件导致的求解困难，提出了一种预条件近端Barzilai-Borwein方法，通过目标级Barzilai-Borwein缩放与共享预条件子结合，并设计子空间变体以避免非对角度量近端映射，在非凸设置下证明了全局收敛性和线性收敛率，实验验证了在ℓ1正则化、结构化ℓ1正则化和线性约束问题上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标复合优化问题在稀疏正则化、约束多目标模型和多任务学习中广泛出现，但现有近端梯度法收敛缓慢，而近端牛顿/拟牛顿法虽利用曲率信息却需计算昂贵的度量近端映射，现有方法难以同时兼顾计算效率与收敛速度。

Method: 提出预条件近端Barzilai-Borwein方法：(1) 目标级Barzilai-Borwein缩放平衡各目标差异，结合公共预条件子捕获共享曲率信息；(2) 设计子空间变体，在由近端梯度型方向和投影历史方向生成的二维子空间中计算搜索方向，通过构造共轭基将子空间模型分解为可处理的一维子问题；(3) 针对g_i(Ax)形式的非光滑项，通过线性算子感知的预条件子导出对偶子问题实现显式近端评估；(4) 提出基于松弛下降条件的非精确版本，分析非凸下的全局收敛性和误差界条件下的线性收敛率。

Result: 在病态ℓ1正则化、结构化ℓ1正则化和线性约束问题上的数值实验验证了该方法相比现有方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在避免非对角度量近端映射计算开销的同时，利用曲率信息加速收敛，理论保证了非凸下的收敛性，实验表明在病态多目标复合优化中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Multiobjective composite optimization problems arise in sparse regularization, constrained multiobjective models, and multi-task learning, but their numerical solution remains challenging when the smooth components are ill-conditioned. Proximal gradient methods are inexpensive per iteration but may converge slowly, while proximal Newton and quasi-Newton methods exploit curvature information at the cost of evaluating expensive metric proximal mappings. To address these issues, we propose a preconditioned proximal Barzilai--Borwein method for multiobjective composite optimization. The method combines objective-wise Barzilai--Borwein scaling, which reduces imbalance among objectives, with a common preconditioner that captures shared curvature information. To avoid non-diagonal metric proximal mappings, we develop a subspace variant in which the search direction is computed in a two-dimensional subspace generated by a proximal-gradient-type direction and a projected historical direction. By constructing a conjugate basis with respect to the preconditioning metric, the subspace model decomposes into tractable one-dimensional subproblems. The framework is further extended to nonsmooth terms of the form $g_i(Ax)$ through a linear-operator-aware preconditioner, yielding explicit proximal evaluations via dual subproblems. We also analyze an inexact version based on relaxed descent conditions. We establish the global convergence of the inexact algorithm in the nonconvex setting and prove a linear convergence rate under an error-bound condition. Numerical experiments on ill-conditioned $\ell_1$-regularized, structured $\ell_1$-regularized, and linearly constrained problems demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [157] [Separation, Constraint Qualifications, and Cycling in Outer Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26897)
*Erik Tamm,Jan Kronqvist*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了外逼近算法在求解凸混合整数非线性规划时两个常被忽视的假设——约束规格成立和非线性规划子问题可精确求解——与循环现象之间的联系，并提出了使用扩展切割平面作为回退策略，在放松约束规格假设下实现有限步收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外逼近算法是求解凸混合整数非线性规划的常用方法，但其收敛性所依赖的某些假设（约束规格成立、子问题可精确求解）在文献中很少被讨论。作者旨在揭示这些假设与算法循环现象之间的内在联系，并寻求在更宽松条件下仍能保证收敛的改进方案。

Method: 1）理论分析约束规格失效和子问题近似求解如何导致分离失败进而引发循环；2）证明当Slater条件接近被违反时，可在精确解附近找到分离失败的点；3）提出在检测到循环时使用扩展切割平面作为回退策略，并证明该策略在放松约束规格假设下仍能保证有限步收敛。

Result: 证明了约束规格失效时，当前迭代点与可行集之间的分离无法保证，导致算法停滞；子问题近似求解同样可能引发分离失败，且某些情况下需要高精度才能避免。当Slater条件接近违反时，精确解附近存在分离失败的点。扩展切割平面回退策略在放松的约束规格假设下实现了有限步收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了外逼近算法中循环现象的深层原因，并通过扩展切割平面策略推广了算法的收敛理论，使其在更宽松的约束规格条件下仍能保证有限步收敛，增强了算法的理论完备性和实际适用性。

Abstract: The outer approximation algorithm is a widely used method for solving convex mixed-integer nonlinear programs. While the algorithm is well established in theory and practice, certain assumptions underlying its convergence are rarely discussed in the literature. In this paper, we examine two such assumptions: that a constraint qualification holds at the optimal solution of each nonlinear programming subproblem, and that these subproblems can be solved exactly. We argue that both assumptions are connected to the issue of cycling, by which we mean that the same integer assignment reappears in successive iterations of the algorithm. When a constraint qualification fails, separation of the current iterate from the feasible set is not guaranteed, which can cause the algorithm to stall. When the nonlinear programming subproblem is solved only approximately, separation may likewise fail, and we show that high precision can be required in certain cases. To formalize the connection between these issues, we prove that when Slater's condition is nearly violated, a point close to the exact solution can be found at which separation fails. Furthermore, within the outer approximation algorithm, we propose to use extended cutting planes as a fallback strategy when cycling is detected. We prove that this approach yields a finitely convergent algorithm under relaxed assumptions regarding constraint qualifications, thereby generalizing the convergence theory of the outer approximation algorithm.

</details>


### [158] [Numerical Approximation for Path-Dependent McKean-Vlasov Control with Non-Asymptotic Error Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27181)
*Olivier Bokanowski,Jean-Francois Chassagneux,Xinyu Li,Christoph Reisinger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对路径依赖的McKean-Vlasov控制问题，提出了一套统一的近似与学习框架，包括Euler离散化、动态规划原理、粒子系统近似以及神经网络策略梯度方法，并给出了非渐近误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有McKean-Vlasov控制理论大多假设系统是马尔可夫的（即无历史依赖），而许多实际应用（如金融、生物群体）中个体的动态和成本函数依赖于历史路径。本文旨在填补路径依赖情形下连续时间MKV控制问题的近似与学习理论空白。

Method: 1) 采用分段常数控制的Euler离散化方案，给出$O(h^{1/4})$的非渐近误差界；2) 建立离散动态规划原理，证明开环控制与历史依赖反馈控制之间的值等价性，从而允许在缩减滤子上优化；3) 引入交互粒子系统近似连续时间值函数，总误差界为$O(h^{1/4}) + O(M^{-γ})$；4) 提出基于路径特征的全可实现神经网络策略梯度方法。

Result: 得到了离散化误差$O(h^{1/4})$和粒子近似误差$O(M^{-γ})$，并通过路径依赖线性二次型基准实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为路径依赖的连续时间平均场控制问题提供了首个统一的理论近似保证与可实现的深度学习求解框架，拓展了MKV控制理论的应用范围。

Abstract: Path-dependent McKean--Vlasov (MKV) control models large interacting populations with history-dependent dynamics and costs. This paper develops a unified approximation-and-learning framework for continuous time path-dependent MKV problem under open-loop controls. First, an Euler discretization scheme with piecewise-constant controls is shown to achieve a non-asymptotic error of $O(h^{1/4})$. Second, we establish a discrete dynamic programming principle and prove value equivalence between open-loop and history-dependent feedback controls, enabling optimization on a reduced filtration. Third, an interacting particle system is introduced to approximate the continuous-time value, yielding an overall error bound of $O(h^{1/4}) + O(M^{-γ})$ for $M$ particles and an explicitly given $γ> 0$. Finally, we propose a fully implementable neural-network policy-gradient method using pathwise features. Numerical experiments, including a path-dependent linear-quadratic benchmark, demonstrate the effectiveness of the algorithm.

</details>


### [159] [Benign Landscape of Quadratic Programs with Orthogonality Constraints and Its Application to Heteroscedastic Probabilistic PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27189)
*Peng Wang,Po Chen,Rujun Jiang,Laura Balzano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带正交约束的齐次二次规划(QPOC)的优化景观，证明其每个临界点要么是全局最大值点，要么是严格鞍点。并将该理论应用于异方差概率主成分分析(HePPCA)，证明其总体版本是QPOC的特例，具有良好的优化景观，且在大样本下样本版本也以高概率继承这些性质，为基于收缩的优化方法的全局线性收敛提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QPOC和HePPCA的优化景观特性，为优化算法的全局收敛性提供理论保证。

Method: 通过闭式刻画临界点集，推导全局最优性的充要条件，证明非最优临界点具有正曲率方向；将HePPCA的总体版本转化为QPOC特例进行分析，并证明局部测地线强凹性；利用大样本理论将性质推广到样本版本。

Result: QPOC的每个临界点要么是全局最大值点，要么是严格鞍点；HePPCA总体版本是QPOC特例，具有良性优化景观且在全局最大值点附近满足局部测地线强凹性；大样本下样本版本以高概率继承这些性质。

Conclusion: 研究成果为QPOC和HePPCA中基于收缩的优化方法收敛到全局解提供了理论依据，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: In this work, we study the optimization landscape of homogeneous quadratic programs with orthogonality constraints (QPOC) and apply the resulting theory to heteroscedastic probabilistic PCA (HePPCA). For QPOC, we establish a complete characterization of the benign optimization landscape by showing that every critical point is either a global maximizer or a strict saddle point. Our analysis builds on a closed-form characterization of the critical point set, from which we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for global optimality and show that every non-optimal critical point has a direction of positive curvature. As an application, we show that the population version of HePPCA is a special instance of QPOC and therefore has a benign optimization landscape; moreover, it satisfies local geodesic strong concavity near every global maximizer. We furthermore prove that, when the sample size is sufficiently large, the sample version of HePPCA inherits these favorable properties with high probability. Together with existing theory on avoiding strict saddle points, our results provide a theoretical justification for the observed local linear convergence of retraction-based optimization methods to global solutions for both QPOC and HePPCA. Finally, we present numerical experiments to corroborate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [160] [Optimizing Accessibility and Maximum Travel Times in Transit Networks: The Hub Covering Location Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27196)
*Carmen-Ana Domínguez-Bravo,Elena Fernández,Armin Lüer-Villagra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化模型，支持综合交通系统设计决策以改善城市出行，包括两种混合整数线性规划公式和一种数学启发式方法，并通过计算实验评估其效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改善城市出行，支持综合交通系统设计中的决策制定。

Method: 提出两种混合整数线性规划公式和一种数学启发式方法，通过计算实验评估效率。

Result: 研究了所得解的结构，分析了所考虑参数的影响。

Conclusion: 该优化模型能有效支持综合交通系统设计决策，通过参数分析揭示了解结构特征。

Abstract: This work introduces an optimization model to support decision-making in the design of integrated transit systems, with the aim of improving urban mobility. We assume that daily user trips consist of two types of segments: short segments where users apply their own transport between their homes or workplaces and the public transit access points, and a longer segment on the transportation network. Users' accessibility is ensured by imposing an upper limit on the travel times of the short segments. We assume that only users within a given coverage radius will employ the system. On the other hand, system effectiveness is ensured by limiting travel time on the transportation network. Two mixed-integer linear programming formulations and a matheuristic procedure are proposed, and their efficiency is evaluated through computational experiments. The results allow us to study the structure of the solutions obtained and analyze the effects of the parameters considered.

</details>


### [161] [Conjecture About Arbitrary Even-Order Convexity of $w$-Optimization of the Split CIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27260)
*Hao Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对数据融合工具Split CIF中的w-优化问题，指出其具有二阶和四阶凸性，并提出一个猜想：该问题可能具有任意偶数阶凸性（即二阶、四阶、六阶……），尽管高阶凸性对实际应用意义不大，但该猜想有望激发有价值的数学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Split CIF是一种用于通用数据融合的重要工具，其核心的w-优化问题已被证明具有二阶凸性（常规凸性）和四阶凸性，这保证了Split CIF的高效实现。作者基于观察和分析，提出该问题可能具有更高阶的偶数凸性，旨在推动相关数学理论的研究。

Method: 本文通过理论观察和分析，对w-优化问题的凸性性质进行总结与推断，提出该问题可能具有任意偶数阶凸性的猜想，并对该猜想进行了详细阐述。

Result: 已知w-优化问题具有二阶凸性和四阶凸性；作者提出猜想认为该问题可能具有任意偶数阶凸性（六阶、八阶等）。

Conclusion: w-优化问题的高阶凸性（四阶及以上）对实际应用（如Split CIF的快速实现）已足够，但任意偶数阶凸性的猜想具有数学理论研究的潜在价值，可能推动系统性数学方法的发展。

Abstract: The $w$-optimization problem is a mathematical problem abstracted from a useful tool for general data fusion rooted in various engineering tasks. Purely from the perspective of practical applications for this useful tool namely the split covariance intersection filter (Split CIF), it is sufficient to know that the $w$-optimization problem enjoys not only the second-order convexity (namely convexity in conventional sense) but also the fourth-order convexity, thanks to which a guaranteed fast implementation of the Split CIF can be realized. On the other hand, based on certain observations and analysis, the author proposes a conjecture concerning the $w$-optimization problem for further mathematical study. The conjecture is that the $w$-optimization problem has arbitrary even-order convexity, or in other words, it has the second-order convexity, the fourth-order convexity, the sixth-order convexity, and so on -- As mentioned above, whether the $w$-optimization problem has higher-order convexity or not might be of little interest itself for practical applications, yet the author believes that the conjecture would stimulate study on systematic mathematical techniques that are potentially interesting and valuable -- This paper presents the conjecture in details.

</details>


### [162] [A Verification Theorem for an Optimal Control Problem Governed by the Convective Brinkman--Forchheimer Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27312)
*Sagar Gautam,Manil T. Mohan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二维和三维环面上由对流Brinkman-Forchheimer方程控制的最优控制问题建立了验证定理，推导了Pontryagin最大值原理，并在二维、三维超临界（r∈(3,5]）及临界（r=3且2βμ≥1）情形下发展了反馈验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navier-Stokes型流体模型的控制问题中验证定理和反馈刻画研究较少，而CBF系统因其非线性吸收项需要不同于二维Navier-Stokes方程的规律性框架，现有方法难以直接推广。

Method: 基于强解理论，精细处理非线性吸收项，发展负阶Sobolev空间中的新估计，以及在更强拓扑下的连续依赖性估计，特别是在三维情形下。

Result: 在二维情形、三维超临界情形（r∈(3,5]）以及临界情形（r=3且2βμ≥1）下，严格证明了反馈表征和验证论证。

Conclusion: 验证框架不仅适用于二维情形，也可推广至三维超临界和临界情形，为CBF系统的最优控制提供了完整的理论支撑。

Abstract: This article establishes a verification theorem for an optimal control problem governed by the two- and three-dimensional convective Brinkman--Forchheimer equations on the $d$-dimensional torus, $d\in\{2,3\}$:
  $$\frac{\partial\mathfrak{u}}{\partial t}
  -μΔ\mathfrak{u}
  +(\mathfrak{u}\cdot\nabla)\mathfrak{u}
  +α\mathfrak{u}
  +β|\mathfrak{u}|^{r-1}\mathfrak{u}
  +\nabla\mathfrak{p}
  =\boldsymbol{f},
  \qquad
  \nabla\cdot\mathfrak{u}=0,$$
  where $μ,α,β>0$ and $r\in[1,\infty)$. We derive the Pontryagin maximum principle and develop a verification framework for the associated control problem, a topic that has received comparatively little attention for fluid models of Navier--Stokes type. A major challenge in establishing the verification theorem and the corresponding feedback characterization for the CBF system is that the analysis requires a substantially different regularity framework from that used for the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations. In particular, the present approach relies on strong solution theory, a delicate treatment of the nonlinear absorption term, novel estimates in negative-order Sobolev spaces, and continuous dependence estimates in stronger topologies, especially in the three-dimensional setting. A distinctive feature of the present work is that the verification framework is developed not only in two dimensions, but also in the three-dimensional supercritical regime, corresponding to $r\in(3,5]$, and in the critical case $r=3$ under the condition $2βμ\geq1$. Consequently, the feedback characterization and verification arguments can be rigorously justified in both two and three dimensions.

</details>
